Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the lagrangian object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 321. The moduli space has dimension at most 232 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the lagrangian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^321 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 232-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
